## Round H23, REGISTERED (2026-11-08, before any search is run and before any id is flown): THE LIT TANK ALONE
### 1. Why
Every priced search so far scored the mean of the dark and lit tanks. In H21 the lit tank is where `c02` separates from the fresh champions (`c02` lit gain +0.0908 at -0.04 kW; the champions +0.066 to +0.079), and `c02` was found by searches that saw both tanks. Two things are unmeasured: whether the lit-tank gain is a verdict by itself (paired on the lit tank alone), and what a search that sees ONLY the lit tank finds, at the price and without it, and whether that law carries over to the dark tank it never saw.
> (A) On the lit tank alone, does `c02` beat the autopilot? (B) Does a lit-only priced search beat `c02` on the lit tank? (C) Does it beat H21's dark-and-lit champion `f4`? (D) Does removing the price (lit-only, 0 per kW) buy more lit score? (E) Does the lit-only priced law beat the autopilot on the dark tank it never saw? (F) Does it beat the networks on the lit tank? (G) Does anything go to full throttle?

### 2. The searches, the arms and the audit
**The law, the tank kind and the box are H21's** (H16's law c, d, k, s; box c [0, 16], d [-0.4, 1.0], k [-1.5, 2], s [-4, 2]; (1+1)-ES, 300 iterations, from the zero law, step 0.1 x the box width, clamped, kept when at least the parent's; all four weights free). **The objective (new) is the LIT objective:** LIT(lambda) = the mean lit score - lambda x the mean lit power in kW, on the TRAIN ids, only the lit flight (no dropout event). **Two groups of six searches:** group **L02** (lambda = 0.02; seeds 23001-23006) and group **L00** (lambda = 0; seeds 23011-23016). Train ids 23000-23023 (24), held-out 23100-23139 (40), 160 s. **Champions:** in each group the search with the best held-out LIT value (the first of a tie): **l02** and **l00**.
**The audit:** ids **23200-23399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s, H15's flight. Sixteen arms: `autopilot`; `law0` (must equal the autopilot); `c02` = [2.2035859388880334, 0.8758638340144, -0.6433501233386668, -2], `h16` = [1.2322693581401742, -0.061415416013719054, -0.6, -0.15850584538981266] and `f4` = [4.146894976024462, -0.024771079209019692, -0.42984102259826307, -1.628187076881964] (H21's champion of the full four-weight search, a dark-and-lit law) as fixed laws; `sched*2`; `l02`, `l00`; the 8 shipped networks. The per-id LIT PRICED SCORE of an arm is its lit score - 0.02 x its lit kW; LIT PRICED GAIN its mean paired difference over the autopilot.
**The verdict, registered before anything is run; seven parts joined by `; `. A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id.**
**Part A:** `ON THE LIT TANK C02 <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> THE AUTOPILOT` (lit priced score). **Part B:** `THE LIT-ONLY PRICED SEARCH <...> C02 ON THE LIT TANK` (l02 against `c02`, lit priced score). **Part C:** `THE LIT-ONLY PRICED SEARCH <...> THE DARK-AND-LIT CHAMPION` (l02 against `f4`, lit priced score). **Part D:** `THE UNPRICED LIT SEARCH <...> THE PRICED ONE ON THE LIT SCORE` (l00 against l02, the plain lit score). **Part E:** `THE LIT-ONLY PRICED LAW <...> THE AUTOPILOT ON THE DARK TANK` (l02 against the autopilot, the dark score). **Part F:** `THE LIT-ONLY PRICED SEARCH <...> THE NETWORKS ON THE LIT TANK` (l02 against the per-id mean of the 8 networks, lit priced score). **Part G:** `FULL THROTTLE AT: <l02, l00 in that order joined by ", ", or NONE>` (mean lit thrust command 0.9 or more).

### 3. The checks
(a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; every network is 963 finite weights; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6, both conditions**; **the lit objective of a law equals the mean lit score minus lambda x the mean lit kW recomputed from a flight (a fixture)**; the twelve searches' laws and both champions lie inside the box; the train, held-out and audit ids are disjoint; **each champion has the largest held-out LIT value in its group**; `c02`, `h16` and `f4` are the registered laws; the 8 networks are pairwise different; no search flew a dark flight.

### 4. The lines
`SEARCH L02 / L00`: the six laws each with train and held-out LIT values and accepted counts, and the champion; `SPREAD`; `AUDIT`: lit and dark mean score, lit mean power and mean lit thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `h16`, `f4`, `sched*2`, `l02`, `l00` and the networks' mean; `LIT PRICED GAIN`; `PAIRED` (the six pairings: mean, se, counts, reading); `EDGE`: each champion's weights and the ones at an edge; `POWER`: lit mean kW, extra kW and efficiency of each champion; `VERDICT`; `LOST SUBS`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** the dark-and-lit priced gain of `l02` and `l00` (to set beside H21's); each champion's dark gain and dark extra power.

## Round H22, RESULT (2026-11-08): c02 IS IN THE MIDDLE OF THE TWENTY SEARCHES ON THEIR OWN HELD-OUT IDS AND 0.006 TO 0.008 ABOVE THE BEST AND THE MEDIAN ON THE AUDIT -- BECAUSE IT SPENDS HALF THE POWER
Searched by `node tools/ship/headToHead22Cmd.mjs search --workers 3` (twenty (1+1)-ES searches of 300 iterations, all four weights free, 0.02 per kW, seeds 22001-22020; train ids 22000-22023, held-out 22100-22139) and flown by `... fly --parts 4` on the audit ids 22200-22399, 160 s, dark and lit, read once; all eleven checks PASS. Data: `brain/rig-results/head-to-head22-search.jsonl`, `brain/rig-results/head-to-head22.json` (0.4 MB). **Verdict as printed: `C02'S HELD-OUT VALUE IS REACHED BY 9 OF 20 SEARCHES; THE BEST SEARCH IS BEHIND C02; THE MEDIAN SEARCH IS BEHIND C02; THE WORST SEARCH BEATS THE AUTOPILOT; SEARCHES IN C02'S BASIN: 1 OF 20; S AT THE BOX EDGE: 0 OF 20`.**
**The distribution.** Held-out priced values of the twenty run from 0.7517 (seed 22020) to 0.7363 (22006); best minus worst 0.0153; rank 5 0.7490, rank 10 0.7445, rank 15 0.7427. `c02` scored on the same held-out ids: 0.7460, which is rank 10 among the twenty and itself (nine searches above it, eleven below). Over the twenty (min / median / max): c 2.04 / 3.30 / 5.87; d -0.23 / -0.05 / 0.64; k -0.59 / -0.31 / 0.27; s -2.54 / -1.06 / -0.35. `c02` is [2.20, 0.876, -0.643, -2]: its c is within the searches' range, **its d (0.876) is above every search's (largest 0.639, seed 22009) and its s (-2) is below all but one (22009, -2.54)**. One search (22004: c 2.51, d 0.31) lies in the registered basin (c 1.2 to 4.0, d at least 0.3); none has `s` on the edge of [-4, 2] (the most negative is -2.54, against an edge at -3.94).
**The audit (dark / lit mean score; dark power).** Autopilot 0.7628 / 0.7741, 3.85 kW. `c02` 0.8524 / 0.8615, 5.03 kW. `h16` 0.8658 / 0.8712, 6.82 kW. best (seed 22020) 0.8601 / 0.8682, 5.88 kW. median (22014) 0.8623 / 0.8667, 5.98 kW. worst (22006) 0.8625 / 0.8771, 6.61 kW.
**Gains (dark / lit), extra power (dark / lit kW), PRICED gain at 0.02.** `c02` +0.0896 / +0.0874, +1.19 / +0.05, **+0.0762**. best +0.0973 / +0.0941, +2.03 / +0.56, +0.0698. median +0.0995 / +0.0925, +2.14 / +0.63, +0.0684. worst +0.0997 / +0.1029, +2.76 / +1.14, +0.0623. `h16` +0.1030 / +0.0971, +2.97 / +1.21, +0.0582.
**Paired on the priced score.** best minus `c02` -0.0063 (se 0.0026; 79 ids better, 121 worse) WORSE. median minus `c02` -0.0078 (se 0.0026; 81 / 119) WORSE. worst minus the autopilot +0.0623 (se 0.0058; 174 / 26) BETTER.
**What it says.** (1) **`c02` is not an outlier of what this recipe finds.** On the held-out ids nine of the twenty searches are at or above it and eleven below; on the audit the best-of-twenty is 0.0063 behind it and the median search 0.0078 behind (2.4 and 3.0 se), against 0.013 to 0.022 behind for H21's three champions. So H21's reading, that `c02` is better than a usual search, holds on the audit but is much smaller than it looked. (2) **The held-out ranking did not predict the audit ranking.** The search that ended on top of the held-out values (22020, 0.7517) is 0.0057 above `c02` there and 0.0063 below it on 200 other ids; the 40 held-out ids are a noisy selector. (3) **What `c02` has that the searches do not is power.** The three flown searches have HIGHER dark and lit gains than `c02` (dark +0.097 to +0.100 against +0.090, lit +0.093 to +0.103 against +0.087) on about twice the extra power (average of dark and lit extra kW 1.3 to 2.0 against 0.6). At 0.02 per kW the extra power costs more than the extra gain buys: best's gain is 0.0072 higher on the dark-and-lit mean and its extra power 0.67 kW higher (0.0134 priced), which is the -0.0062. (4) Every one of the twenty beats the autopilot on the worst-case check (the worst is +0.0623, 174 ids of 200), so the family is a gain over the autopilot whatever the seed; the spread between seeds (0.0153 held-out) is small beside that gain.

E: basin 1 of 20 was in the 35 percent branch (1-3; none 55); `s` at the edge 0 of 20 held (75). `c02`'s held-out value above the median of the twenty (85): held (0.7460 against 0.74445, the mean of ranks 10 and 11). **The spread (best minus worst) above 0.02 (60 percent): did not hold (0.0153).**
**What this does NOT say.** It does not say `c02` is the optimum or that a search cannot beat it: twenty seeds of one recipe at 300 iterations, one price, one box, and the best of twenty (by a noisy 40-id selector) is behind it on the audit by a margin that three flown laws cannot tell from a draw. Only the best, the median and the worst were flown; the other seventeen were read on held-out ids only, and the held-out ranking was a poor predictor of the audit one, so which searches would beat `c02` on the audit is not known. `c02` was itself the best of four searches in H18 on other ids, so its audit values here (+0.0762) and in H21 (+0.0910) and H18 (+0.0837) differ by the id set by up to 0.015. The price (0.02 per kW) decides the reading: at a lower price the searches' higher gains would count for more. One id set, one tank pair; the lit tank alone is H23.

## Round H24, REGISTERED (2026-11-08, before anything is built or flown): s WIDENED, c02-big AGAINST c02 ON FRESH IDS, AND l02 AGAINST c02 ON THE DARK TANK
### 1. Why
Three loose ends, one audit. (a) **`s` widened.** H21 widened the searched range of `s` from [-2, 2] to [-4, 2] because three of H18's searches ended on -2; in H21 and H22 no search ended near either edge (the most negative of H22's twenty was -2.54). That says the searches do not want to go far, not what the priced score does out there: `c02` sits at `s` = -2 and H20 showed `s` holds its power down (without it +19.84 kW). So this round scans `s` on `c02`'s own law, out to -8, with the other three weights held. (b) **c02-big against c02.** H20 found `c02`'s large-error half alone (its thrust only where the pilot's heading error is above 0.2 rad, the autopilot's elsewhere) keeps 97 percent of `c02`'s dark gain on twice its extra power; that was read on H20's ids against the autopilot, not paired against `c02` on a fresh set. (c) **l02 against c02 on the dark tank.** H23's lit-only champion has a dark gain over the autopilot (+0.1133) but was never paired against `c02` on the dark tank, and I have already SEEN the H23 means (dark score 0.8837 against 0.8777, dark power 5.47 against 4.91 kW), so the expectations for (c) are informed by them; the fresh ids below are what make the reading a test.
> (A) Does any other `s` beat `c02`'s -2 on the priced score? (B) Does `c02-big` beat or tie `c02`? (C) Does l02 beat or tie `c02` on the dark tank?

### 2. The arms and the audit
**No search is run.** Every arm is a fixed law. **The tank kind and flight are H15's, H16's law kind and H20's mixed kind.** Thirteen arms: `autopilot`; `law0` (the zero law: must equal the autopilot); `c02` = [2.2035859388880334, 0.8758638340144, -0.6433501233386668, -2]; **the `s` scan: `c02` with only `s` replaced, nine values in all (eight arms plus `c02` itself at -2): `s-8`, `s-6`, `s-4`, `s-3`, `s-2.5`, `s-1.5`, `s-1`, `s0`** (c = 2.2035859388880334, d = 0.8758638340144, k = -0.6433501233386668 in each); `c02-big` = H20's arm (the autopilot's channels, with `c02`'s law as the thrust source, used only where the pilot's own heading error is above 0.2 rad; the autopilot's thrust elsewhere); `l02` = [1.5285188545844506, 0.14215273999243602, -0.5822553986190563, -0.6377199326759313] (H23's champion). **The audit:** ids **24200-24399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s. The per-id PRICED SCORE is (dark score + lit score)/2 - 0.02 x (dark kW + lit kW)/2; PRICED GAIN its mean paired difference over the autopilot; the DARK PRICED SCORE is the dark score - 0.02 x the dark kW.
**The verdict, registered before anything is built or run; seven parts joined by `; `. A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id.**
**Part A1:** `THE BEST OF THE MORE NEGATIVE s (<value>) <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> C02`: among `s-8`, `s-6`, `s-4`, `s-3`, `s-2.5` the arm with the largest priced gain (the first of a tie, in that order), against `c02`, on the priced score. **Part A2:** `THE BEST OF THE LESS NEGATIVE s (<value>) <...> C02`: among `s-1.5`, `s-1`, `s0` likewise. **Part A3:** `THE BEST s OF THE SCAN IS <value>`: the scan value (the nine, `c02`'s -2 included) with the largest priced gain, the first of a tie in the order -8, -6, -4, -3, -2.5, -2, -1.5, -1, 0. (A1 and A2 read the best of a group on the SAME ids, so a BEATS is biased upward and would need fresh ids to stand; a TIES or IS BEHIND is not biased by it.)
**Part B1:** `C02-BIG <...> C02` on the priced score. **Part B2:** `C02-BIG <...> C02 ON THE DARK SCORE` (the plain dark score).
**Part C1:** `L02 <...> C02 ON THE DARK SCORE` (the plain dark score). **Part C2:** `L02 <...> C02 ON THE DARK PRICED SCORE` (dark score - 0.02 x dark kW). (The dark-and-lit priced score of l02 against `c02` is reported, not in the verdict.)
(That is A1, A2, A3, B1, B2, C1, C2: seven parts.)

### 3. The checks
(a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6, both conditions**; **each scan arm's law is `c02`'s with only `s` changed, to the registered value (the eight laws, in order)**; `c02`, `c02-big` and `l02` are the registered laws and `c02-big`'s band is 0.2 rad; **`c02-big` is different from `c02` and from the autopilot in the dark; the eight scan arms and `c02` are pairwise different in the dark**; the audit ids are the registered ones and disjoint from H21's, H22's and H23's.

### 4. The lines
`AUDIT`: dark and lit mean score, dark mean power and mean dark thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `c02-big`, `l02` and the eight scan arms; `PRICED GAIN`; `SCAN`: for each `s` (-8 to 0, `c02`'s -2 in its place) the dark gain, lit gain, dark and lit extra kW and priced gain, then the order of the nine by priced gain; `PAIRED`: the seven pairings (mean, se, counts, reading); `POWER`: dark extra kW and efficiency of `c02`, `c02-big`, `l02` and the scan; `VERDICT`; `LOST SUBS`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** l02 against `c02` on the dark-and-lit priced score; the lit-tank pairing of `c02-big` against `c02` and of l02 against `c02`; `c02-big`'s share of `c02`'s dark gain.

### 5. Expectations, recorded BEFORE anything is built or run
Part A1: TIES 55, IS BEHIND 25, BEATS 20 (biased upward, as above). A2: IS BEHIND 80, TIES 15, BEATS 5. A3: the best `s` is -2 (`c02`'s own) 25 percent, -2.5 20, -3 20, -4 15, -1.5 10, anything else 10. Part B1 (`c02-big` against `c02`, priced): IS BEHIND 85, TIES 12, BEATS 3. B2 (dark score): TIES 50, IS BEHIND 35, BEATS 15. Part C1 (l02 against `c02`, dark score): TIES 55, BEATS 35, IS BEHIND 10. C2 (dark priced): TIES 45, IS BEHIND 45, BEATS 10. The arm `s0` has a dark extra power of at least 10 kW over the autopilot: 85 percent. The arm `s-8` has a priced gain at least 0.02 below `c02`'s: 60 percent. `c02-big`'s dark extra power is at least 1.5 times `c02`'s: 80 percent. `c02-big` keeps at least 90 percent of `c02`'s dark gain: 70 percent. The priced gain of l02 on the dark-and-lit measure is within 0.01 of `c02`'s: 75 percent.

### 6. What will be built
`tools/ship/headToHead24.mjs` (the arms: the scan laws, `c02-big` from H20's mixer, `l02`; the audit using H15's flight; the priced and dark-priced readings, the scan table and ordering, checks, verdict and lines; reads nothing from disk), `headToHead24Cmd.mjs` (`fly` mode: parallel parts, `--from-json`; no search mode) and `headToHead24-selfcheck.mjs` (with a mutation pass). No search is run: the scan answers the `s` question directly where a re-search could not (no search of H21 or H22 ended past -2.54), and a wider-box search is left as a later round if the scan finds a rise beyond -2.

**What it says.** (1) **`c02`'s `s` = -2 is the best of the nine, and it is a knee.** On the more negative side the gain falls faster than the power: from -2 to -2.5 the dark gain falls 0.044 and the lit gain 0.054 for 1.8 kW (dark) and 1.3 kW (lit) less; at -3 the dark gain is +0.0065 on a power 1.6 kW below the autopilot's; from -4 down the arm is below the autopilot on both tanks, and at -8 and -6 the thrust is negative (reverse) and subs are lost. (3) **`c02-big`'s gains are larger than `c02`'s in both tanks** (dark +0.0092, 2.2 se; lit +0.0117, 3.3 se) **but its extra power is larger still** (dark +2.14 against +1.25 kW, 1.7 times; lit +1.38 against +0.07 kW), so on the priced score it is 0.0115 behind (3.2 se). Read as a transplant: `c02`'s settled half (its thrust where the heading error is 0.2 rad or less) costs 0.009 of dark score and 0.012 of lit score on these ids and saves about 1.1 kW on average; at 0.02 per kW that is 0.022 saved for 0.010 given up. (4) **l02 ties `c02` on the dark tank**: by score (+0.0045, 1.2 se) and by dark priced score (-0.0047, 1.2 se) and, reported, on the dark-and-lit priced score (-0.0020). H23's reading on its own ids (a law searched on the lit tank only carries over to the dark tank at `c02`'s level) holds on fresh ids. On the lit tank l02 is ahead by +0.0079 on the score at 0.4 kW against 0.07 kW lit extra power.
**Break-even prices, from the printed means (arithmetic, not a run).** From `s` -2 to -1.5 the mean of the dark and lit gains rises by 0.0300 and the mean extra power by 2.55 kW: `s` -1.5 beats -2 only below about 0.012 per kW. From -2 to -2.5 the mean gain falls 0.0491 and the mean extra power falls 1.54 kW: -2.5 beats -2 only above about 0.032 per kW. So at this id set `c02`'s -2 is the best `s` for prices from roughly 0.012 to 0.032 per kW, and 0.02 is inside that band.
**Against the registered expectations.** A1: IS BEHIND was my 25 percent branch (TIES 55, BEATS 20). A2: IS BEHIND (80) held. A3: -2 was my largest single pick (25 percent; -2.5 and -3 20 each). B1: IS BEHIND (85) held. **B2: BEATS was my 15 percent branch (TIES 50, IS BEHIND 35).** C1: TIES (55) held. C2: TIES (45; IS BEHIND 45) held. `s0` dark extra power at least 10 kW (85): held (+19.75). `s-8` priced gain at least 0.02 below `c02`'s (60): held (0.251 below). `c02-big` dark extra power at least 1.5 times `c02`'s (80): held (1.7). `c02-big` keeps at least 90 percent of `c02`'s dark gain (70): held (109). l02 dark-and-lit priced gain within 0.01 of `c02`'s (75): held (0.0020).
**What this does NOT say.** It does not say `s` = -2 is the optimum of the law: the scan holds `c`, `d` and `k` at `c02`'s values and moves only `s` on a grid of 0.5, so the knee is located to within one step (a value between -2.25 and -1.75 might be a hair better) and a joint re-optimisation of the other weights at another `s` was not run (H21's and H22's searches with `s` free in [-4, 2] ended no further than -2.54). The reading of the two best-of-group parts is of the best arm of a group on the SAME ids, which can only flatter it; both are IS BEHIND, so the bias does not change the answer. The break-even prices are arithmetic on one id set's means, with no standard error. The comparison of `c02-big` with `c02` is one id set, and which half of `c02` is worth its power depends on the price (at 0.012 per kW or less `c02-big` would be ahead, by the same arithmetic). l02 is one law against one law on one id set: TIES at 2 se is not equality, and its lit-tank lead (+0.0079, 3.8 se) is real but not in the verdict.

## Round H25, REGISTERED (2026-11-08, before anything is built, searched or flown): A PRICE SCAN OF THE WHOLE FAMILY
### 1. Why
Everything since H18 has been read at one price, 0.02 per kW. H24's arithmetic put `c02`'s `s` = -2 as the best `s` for prices between about 0.012 and 0.032 per kW, which says the answer depends on the price and that 0.02 sits in the middle of that band. H18 did search at 0.01, 0.02 and 0.04 (four searches each, in a box with `s` no lower than -2), but never audited all the champions at all the prices, never put the networks and `sched*2` beside them at every price, and never went below 0.01. This round searches the full four-weight family at four prices, **0.005, 0.01, 0.02 and 0.04 per kW**, six searches each, in H21's box (`s` in [-4, 2]), and audits the four champions with `c02`, `h16`, `sched*2` and the eight networks on 200 fresh ids, reading every arm at every price.
> (A) Does the champion's extra power fall as the price rises? (B) At each price does the searched law beat `c02`? (C) Which arm is best at each price? (D) At the lowest price, does the best network beat the searched law? (E) Does anything go to full throttle or end on an edge of `s`?

### 2. The searches, the arms and the audit
**The law, tank kind, box and search recipe are H21's** (H16's law c, d, k, s; box c [0, 16], d [-0.4, 1.0], k [-1.5, 2], s [-4, 2]; (1+1)-ES, 300 iterations, from the zero law, all four weights free, step 0.1 x the box width, clamped, kept when at least the parent's). **The objective (new in its price only) is H18's:** PRICED(lambda) = (the mean dark score + the mean lit score)/2 - lambda x (the mean dark power + the mean lit power)/2 in kW, on the TRAIN ids. **Four groups of six searches:** **P005** (lambda 0.005; seeds 25001-25006), **P01** (0.01; 25011-25016), **P02** (0.02; 25021-25026), **P04** (0.04; 25031-25036). Train ids 25000-25023 (24), held-out 25100-25139 (40), 160 s. **Champions:** in each group the search with the best held-out value AT THAT GROUP'S PRICE (the first of a tie in the seed order): `p005`, `p01`, `p02`, `p04`.
**The audit:** ids **25200-25399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s, H15's flight. Seventeen arms: `autopilot`; `law0` (the zero law: must equal the autopilot); `c02` = [2.2035859388880334, 0.8758638340144, -0.6433501233386668, -2] and `h16` = [1.2322693581401742, -0.061415416013719054, -0.6, -0.15850584538981266] as fixed laws; `sched*2`; the four champions; the 8 shipped networks. **Every arm is read at every price:** the per-id PRICED SCORE of an arm at a price lambda is (dark score + lit score)/2 - lambda x (dark kW + lit kW)/2; its PRICED GAIN at that price its mean paired difference over the autopilot. **EXTRA POWER** of an arm is the mean of its dark and lit extra kW over the autopilot's.
**The verdict, registered before anything is run; twelve parts joined by `; `. A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id on the per-id priced score at the stated price.**
**Part A:** `EXTRA POWER FALLS AS THE PRICE RISES: <YES/NO>`: YES when the champions' extra power is strictly ordered p005 > p01 > p02 > p04.
**Parts B1-B4:** `AT <0.005/0.01/0.02/0.04> THE SEARCHED LAW <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> C02`: the champion of that price against `c02`, on the priced score at that price.
**Parts C1-C4:** `BEST AT <price>: <arm>`: the arm among all but `autopilot` and `law0` with the largest priced gain at that price (the first of a tie in the order `c02`, `h16`, `sched*2`, `p005`, `p01`, `p02`, `p04`, then the networks in H9's order); the arm's name as above (`c02`, `h16`, `sched*2`, `p005`, ..., `network-<name>`).
**Part D:** `AT 0.005 THE BEST NETWORK <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> THE SEARCHED LAW`: the network with the largest priced gain at 0.005 against `p005`, on the priced score at 0.005.
**Part E:** `FULL THROTTLE AT: <the champions in the order p005, p01, p02, p04 whose mean dark thrust is 0.9 or more, joined by ", ", or NONE>; S AT THE BOX EDGE: <the champions whose `s` is within 1 percent of the s-width (0.06) of -4 or 2, or NONE>` (counted as two items of the twelve).
(That is A, B1-B4, C1-C4, D, E1, E2: twelve.)

### 3. The checks
(a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; every network is 963 finite weights; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6, both conditions**; exactly 24 searches, six seeds in each group once, every law and champion inside the box; **each champion has the largest held-out value, at its own price, in its group**; the train, held-out and audit ids are disjoint from each other; `c02` and `h16` are the registered laws; the 8 networks are pairwise different; **the objective at 0.02 equals H21's objective on the same law and ids (a fixture, to the last digit) and at 0 equals the plain mean of the dark and lit scores**.

### 4. The lines
`SEARCH P005 / P01 / P02 / P04`: the six laws of each with train and held-out values and accepted counts, and the champion; `SPREAD`: the largest minus the smallest held-out value per group; `AUDIT`: dark and lit mean score, dark power and mean dark thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `h16`, `sched*2`, the champions and the networks' mean; `EXTRA POWER`: each arm's extra power; `PRICED GAIN`: a table, arms by prices (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.04), with the networks' mean and range; `PAIRED`: the five pairings (mean, se, counts, reading); `EDGE`: each champion's weights and the ones at an edge; `VERDICT`; `LOST SUBS`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** each champion's dark and lit gain; the break-even price between each pair of neighbouring champions (the gain difference over the power difference); the champions' weights as a path (c, d, k, s against the price).

### 5. Expectations, recorded BEFORE anything is run
Part A: YES 65 percent (six seeds per group leave room for a swap between neighbours). Parts B: at 0.005 BEATS 70, TIES 25, IS BEHIND 5; at 0.01 BEATS 45, TIES 40, IS BEHIND 15; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 45, TIES 40, BEATS 15 (H22's typical search was 0.006 to 0.008 behind); at 0.04 BEATS 40, TIES 40, IS BEHIND 20. Parts C: best at 0.005: `p005` 55, `sched*2` 20, `h16` 10, a network 10, `c02` 5; at 0.01: `p01` 45, `c02` 20, `h16` 15, `sched*2` 10, `p005` 5, other 5; at 0.02: `c02` 35, `p02` 30, `p01` 15, `h16` 10, other 10; at 0.04: `p04` 40, `p02` 25, `c02` 20, other 15. Part D: the best network IS BEHIND 75, TIES 20, BEATS 5. Part E: FULL THROTTLE NONE 70 percent; S AT THE BOX EDGE: NONE 45 percent. `p005`'s extra power is at least twice `p02`'s: 60 percent. `p04`'s extra power is below 0.5 kW: 50 percent. The spread of the held-out values in each group (largest minus smallest) is below 0.02: 60 percent per group. `c02` is within 0.01 of the best arm's priced gain at 0.02: 60 percent.

### 6. What will be built
`tools/ship/headToHead25.mjs` (the priced search with a price argument and the four groups, the champions, the audit using H15's flight and H16's law kind, the readings at every price, checks, verdict and lines; imports H21's box and arms; reads nothing from disk), `headToHead25Cmd.mjs` (`search` mode: 24 children writing a jsonl, resumable; `fly` mode: parallel parts with `--from-json`) and `headToHead25-selfcheck.mjs` (with a mutation pass). The search is the long part (about an hour on 4 cores).

## Round H25, RESULT (2026-11-08): EXTRA POWER FALLS WITH THE PRICE, EACH SEARCHED LAW IS BEST AT ITS OWN PRICE, AND c02 STAYS WITHIN 0.016 OF THE BEST AT EVERY PRICE
Searched by `node tools/ship/headToHead25Cmd.mjs search --workers 4` (24 (1+1)-ES searches of 300 iterations, six at each of 0.005, 0.01, 0.02 and 0.04 per kW, all four weights free, in H21's box; train ids 25000-25023, held-out 25100-25139; about 45 minutes on 4 cores, each search about 7 minutes) and flown by `... fly --parts 4` on the audit ids 25200-25399, 160 s, dark and lit, read once (about two minutes); all thirteen checks PASS. Data: `brain/rig-results/head-to-head25-search.jsonl`, `brain/rig-results/head-to-head25.json` (1.0 MB). **Verdict as printed: `EXTRA POWER FALLS AS THE PRICE RISES: YES; AT 0.005 THE SEARCHED LAW BEATS C02; AT 0.01 THE SEARCHED LAW BEATS C02; AT 0.02 THE SEARCHED LAW TIES C02; AT 0.04 THE SEARCHED LAW TIES C02; BEST AT 0.005: p005; BEST AT 0.01: p01; BEST AT 0.02: c02; BEST AT 0.04: p04; AT 0.005 THE BEST NETWORK IS BEHIND THE SEARCHED LAW; FULL THROTTLE AT: NONE; S AT THE BOX EDGE: NONE`.**
**The champions (c d k s), best held-out value of six at their own price.** p005 (seed 25003, held 0.8386) [3.506, 0.729, -0.681, -1.747]. p01 (25016, 0.8072) [2.117, 0.612, -0.710, -1.389]. p02 (25024, 0.7566) [2.882, 0.547, -0.510, -1.768]. p04 (25031, 0.6821) [2.997, 0.087, -0.433, -1.683]. For comparison `c02` is [2.204, 0.876, -0.643, -2]. Held-out spreads (best minus worst of six): 0.0059 / 0.0066 / 0.0172 / 0.0123. **At 0.02 two independent seeds ended on almost the same law**: 25023 [2.889, 0.557, -0.543, -1.682] and 25024 [2.882, 0.547, -0.510, -1.768], held-out 0.7565 and 0.7566, the two best of the six (the next is 0.7530). No champion has `s` near an edge (-1.4 to -1.8).
**The audit (dark / lit mean score; dark power).** Autopilot 0.7523 / 0.7712, 3.70 kW. `c02` 0.8706 / 0.8661, 5.08 kW. `h16` 0.8763 / 0.8844, 6.72 kW. `sched*2` 0.8739 / 0.9005, 8.65 kW. p005 0.8843 / 0.9026, 7.44 kW. p01 0.8813 / 0.8907, 6.32 kW. p02 0.8738 / 0.8690, 5.40 kW. p04 0.8394 / 0.8158, 3.97 kW. The networks' mean 0.8623 / 0.8776, 8.00 kW.
**Extra power (the mean of the dark and lit extra kW) and PRICED gain at 0.005 / 0.01 / 0.02 / 0.04.** `c02` +0.79 kW; +0.1026 / +0.0987 / +0.0908 / +0.0750. `h16` +2.17; +0.1078 / +0.0969 / +0.0752 / +0.0318. `sched*2` +4.23; +0.1043 / +0.0831 / +0.0409 / -0.0437. **p005 +2.73; +0.1181 / +0.1044 / +0.0772 / +0.0226. p01 +1.85; +0.1150 / +0.1057 / +0.0872 / +0.0502. p02 +0.98; +0.1048 / +0.0999 / +0.0901 / +0.0706. p04 -0.33; +0.0674 / +0.0691 / +0.0723 / +0.0789.** The networks' mean +3.75 kW; +0.0895 (range +0.0758 to +0.0997) / +0.0707 (+0.0617 to +0.0780) / +0.0332 (+0.0306 to +0.0358) / -0.0418 (-0.0534 to -0.0229). Lost runs: none in any arm.
**Paired, each at its own price.** p005 minus `c02` at 0.005: +0.0154 (se 0.0024; 156 ids better, 44 worse) BETTER. p01 minus `c02` at 0.01: +0.0070 (se 0.0022; 141 / 59) BETTER. p02 minus `c02` at 0.02: -0.0007 (se 0.0021; 106 / 94) TIES. p04 minus `c02` at 0.04: +0.0039 (se 0.0056; 100 / 100) TIES. The best network (`network-Actuators`) minus p005 at 0.005: -0.0184 (se 0.0040; 65 / 135) WORSE. Reported, not in the verdict: dark / lit gain p005 +0.1320 / +0.1314, p01 +0.1290 / +0.1195, p02 +0.1215 / +0.0978, p04 +0.0871 / +0.0445; the break-even price between neighbouring champions (mean gain difference over mean extra-power difference) p005 to p01 0.0085, p01 to p02 0.0167, p02 to p04 0.0337 per kW.
**What it says.** (1) **The searched family has a frontier in the price.** Extra power falls strictly as the price rises, +2.73, +1.85, +0.98 and -0.33 kW (p04 draws less than the autopilot: dark +0.27 kW, lit -0.92 kW), and the gain falls with it (dark gain +0.132, +0.129, +0.122, +0.087). Each champion is the best arm at its own price except at 0.02, where it is `c02` by 0.0007 over p02. (2) **The weight that moves with the price is `d`**, the constant added to the thrust: 0.73, 0.61, 0.55, then 0.09; `c` (2.1 to 3.5) and `s` (-1.4 to -1.8) show no trend and `k` stays between -0.4 and -0.7. `c02`'s d = 0.876 is above all four champions' while its power (+0.79 kW) is close to p02's (+0.98): its `s` (-2) is the most negative of the five and its `k` (-0.64) is in the middle of the champions' (-0.43 to -0.71). (3) **`c02` is a good compromise.** Its priced gain is within 0.0155 of the best arm at 0.005 (+0.1026 against +0.1181), 0.0070 at 0.01, 0 at 0.02 and 0.0039 at 0.04 (a tie with the champion: se 0.0056); among the fixed laws the largest regret over the four prices is 0.0155 for `c02`, 0.0133 for p02 (at 0.005), 0.0287 for p01 and 0.0471 for `h16` (both at 0.04), 0.0507 for p04 (at 0.005) and 0.0563 for p005 (at 0.04), so only p02, which is nearly the same law, is a hair better by this measure. (4) **The break-even prices say where each law takes over**: p005 below about 0.0085 per kW, p01 from there to about 0.0167, p02 (and `c02`, which ties it) to about 0.0337, p04 above. H24's arithmetic put `c02`'s `s` optimum between 0.012 and 0.032: the two bands agree to within about 0.005. (5) **The networks lose at every price.** At 0.005 the best network is 0.0184 behind p005 and behind `c02` (+0.0997 against +0.1026), `h16` (+0.1078) and `sched*2` (+0.1043); from 0.02 up every network's priced gain is below `c02`'s by 0.05 or more, and at 0.04 all eight are negative (-0.023 to -0.053). (6) The searches are consistent within a price: the six held-out values lie within 0.006 to 0.017 of each other, and at 0.02 two seeds find the same law.
**Against the registered expectations.** A: YES (65 percent) held. B: at 0.005 BEATS (70) held; at 0.01 BEATS (45) held; **at 0.02 TIES was my 40 percent branch** (IS BEHIND 45); **at 0.04 TIES was my 40 percent branch** (BEATS 40). C: best at 0.005 `p005` (55) held, at 0.01 `p01` (45) held, at 0.02 `c02` (35) held, at 0.04 `p04` (40) held: **each was my most likely branch.** D: the best network IS BEHIND (75) held. E: FULL THROTTLE NONE (70) held; S AT THE BOX EDGE NONE (45) held. `p005`'s extra power at least twice `p02`'s (60): held (2.8 times). `p04`'s extra power below 0.5 kW (50): held (-0.33). The held-out spread in each group below 0.02 (60 per group): held in all four. `c02` within 0.01 of the best arm at 0.02 (60): held (it is the best arm).
**What this does NOT say.** It does not say the four champions are the best laws at their prices: each is the best of six searches of 300 iterations chosen on 40 held-out ids, and H22 showed that ranking to be a noisy selector; the margins that matter here (p005 over `c02` +0.0154, p01 over `c02` +0.0070) are several se, but the order of the champions among themselves and against `c02` at 0.02 and 0.04 is within noise (TIES). It does not say the networks are bad at power: they were trained for another objective and were not searched at these prices, so the comparison favours the laws that were. The break-even prices are arithmetic on one id set's means, with no standard error. Only four prices, one box, one law family of four weights, one id set; the lit tank was read as half of the priced score (H23 read it alone), and nothing here separates the tanks.

## Round H26, REGISTERED (2026-11-08, before anything is built, searched or flown): A NETWORK RE-SEARCHED AT A PRICE
### 1. Why
H25 found the networks losing at every price: at 0.02 the best of the eight is +0.0346 against `c02`'s +0.0908, and at 0.04 all eight are negative. It also said, plainly, that the comparison favours the laws, because the networks were never tuned for these prices: they were trained for dives (H5's recipe on a dive TRAIN_SET) or by the robust-hull and tail variants, and the laws were searched on the priced compass-dropout objective itself. So the standing reading "networks lose" is partly "networks were not asked". This round asks: it takes the base-recipe network `network-Wip` (the start of H20's and H11's work, +0.0903 dark gain in H25 on +3.16 kW) and searches its 963 weights with the same (1+1)-ES and the same priced compass-dropout objective the laws had, at three prices (0.005, 0.02 and 0.04 per kW), four searches each, then audits the three champions with `Wip` itself, `c02` and H25's law champions on 200 fresh ids.
> (A) Does searching a network at a price beat the network it started from, at that price? (B) Does it reach `c02`? (C) Does its extra power fall as the price rises? (D) Which arm is best at each price? (E) Does a network go to full throttle?

### 2. The searches, the arms and the audit
**The start and the search.** Each search starts from the weights of `network-Wip` (brain/pilotSubWeightsWip.json, 963 weights, the Giant Fiber Circuit pilot, `pilot: network, circuit: gfc`), so its first scored candidate IS `Wip`. **(1+1)-ES, 600 iterations:** each iteration moves 1 to 4 of the 963 weights by a gaussian of sigma 0.3 (H5's sparse mutation, `PS.mutate`'s: the same sigma and maxK as the base recipe, the 650 outside and 313 inside the recurrent block all free), the child is kept when its TRAIN priced objective is at least the parent's; **no prune** (the base recipe's prune back toward the hand weights is not used: the objective already prices power). **The objective is H18's:** PRICED(lambda) = (the mean dark score + the mean lit score)/2 - lambda x (the mean dark power + the mean lit power)/2 in kW, on the TRAIN ids, H15's flight, 160 s. **Three groups of four searches:** **N005** (lambda 0.005; seeds 26001-26004), **N02** (0.02; 26011-26014), **N04** (0.04; 26021-26024). Train ids 26000-26023 (24), held-out 26100-26139 (40). **Champions:** in each group the search with the best held-out priced value AT THAT GROUP'S PRICE (the first of a tie in the seed order): `n005`, `n02`, `n04`. A search that keeps no child returns `Wip` itself.
**The audit:** ids **26200-26399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s, H15's flight. Ten arms: `autopilot`; `law0` (must equal the autopilot); `c02` = [2.2035859388880334, 0.8758638340144, -0.6433501233386668, -2]; **H25's champion laws `p005` (seed 25003), `p02` (25024) and `p04` (25031) as stored in brain/rig-results/head-to-head25-search.jsonl, to the last digit**; `wip` (`network-Wip`, the start); the three champions `n005`, `n02`, `n04`. Every arm is read at every price: the per-id PRICED SCORE at lambda is (dark score + lit score)/2 - lambda x (dark kW + lit kW)/2; PRICED GAIN its mean paired difference over the autopilot; EXTRA POWER the mean of the dark and lit extra kW over the autopilot's.
**The verdict, registered before anything is run; eleven parts joined by `; `. A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id on the per-id priced score at the stated price.**
**Parts A1-A3:** `AT <0.005/0.02/0.04> THE RE-SEARCHED NETWORK <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> ITS START`: the champion of that price against `wip`, at that price. **Parts B1-B3:** `AT <price> THE RE-SEARCHED NETWORK <...> C02`: the champion against `c02`, at that price. **Part C:** `EXTRA POWER FALLS AS THE PRICE RISES: <YES/NO>`: YES when the champions' extra power is strictly ordered n005 > n02 > n04. **Parts D1-D3:** `BEST AT <price>: <arm>`: the arm among all but `autopilot` and `law0` with the largest priced gain at that price (the first of a tie in the order `c02`, `p005`, `p02`, `p04`, `wip`, `n005`, `n02`, `n04`). **Part E:** `FULL THROTTLE AT: <the champions, in the order n005, n02, n04, whose mean dark thrust is 0.9 or more, or NONE>`.
(That is A1-A3, B1-B3, C, D1-D3, E: eleven.)

### 3. The checks
(a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6, both conditions**; exactly 12 searches, four seeds in each group once, every searched and champion weight vector 963 finite numbers; **the first scored candidate of a search equals `wip` (the start's priced objective on the TRAIN ids, to the last digit; a fixture)**; **each champion has the largest held-out value, at its own price, in its group**; the train, held-out and audit ids are disjoint; `c02` and the three H25 laws are the registered ones; `wip`'s weights are the shipped file's; **the objective at 0.02 equals H21's on the same law and ids and at 0 equals the plain mean of the dark and lit scores** (H25's fixture).

### 4. The lines
`SEARCH N005 / N02 / N04`: the four searches of each with train and held-out value, accepted count, and how many weights differ from `Wip`'s, and the champion; `START`: `wip`'s train and held-out priced value at each price; `SPREAD`: the largest minus the smallest held-out value in each group; `AUDIT`: dark and lit mean score, dark power and mean dark thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `p005`, `p02`, `p04`, `wip` and the champions; `EXTRA POWER`; `PRICED GAIN` (arms by prices 0.005, 0.02, 0.04); `PAIRED` (the six pairings: mean, se, counts, reading); `VERDICT`; `LOST SUBS`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** each champion's dark and lit gain; each champion's change in extra power from `wip`'s; the H25 law champion of the same price against the network champion at that price (paired, priced).

### 5. Expectations, recorded BEFORE anything is run
Parts A: at 0.005 BEATS 55, TIES 40, IS BEHIND 5 (`Wip` is already near what a network does at that price); at 0.02 BEATS 85, TIES 14, IS BEHIND 1; at 0.04 BEATS 90, TIES 9, IS BEHIND 1. Parts B (against `c02`): at 0.005 IS BEHIND 45, TIES 40, BEATS 15; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 70, TIES 25, BEATS 5; at 0.04 IS BEHIND 55, TIES 30, BEATS 15. Part C: YES 70 percent. Parts D: best at 0.005: `p005` 55, `n005` 20, `c02` 15, other 10; at 0.02: `c02` 40, `p02` 30, `n02` 20, other 10; at 0.04: `p04` 45, `n04` 25, `c02` 15, `p02` 10, other 5. Part E: NONE 85 percent. `n02`'s extra power is at least 1 kW below `Wip`'s: 85 percent. `n04`'s extra power is below 1.0 kW: 55 percent. Each champion's held-out priced value is above `Wip`'s at its price: 90 percent each. The four held-out values in a group lie within 0.01 of each other: 50 percent per group. Every search keeps between 20 and 150 of its 600 children: 70 percent.

### 6. What will be built
`tools/ship/headToHead26.mjs` (the network search with a price argument, H5's sparse mutation reproduced from `PS.mutate`, the three groups, the champions, the audit using H15's flight and the network arm, H25's readings at three prices, checks, verdict and lines; imports H18's objective, H21's fixture machinery and H25's readings; reads nothing from disk: the command hands in `Wip`'s weights, the H25 laws and the search rows), `headToHead26Cmd.mjs` (`search` mode: twelve children writing a jsonl, resumable; `fly` mode: parallel parts with `--from-json`) and `headToHead26-selfcheck.mjs` (with a mutation pass). A network flight costs about twice a law's, so each search is about 30 minutes and the twelve about an hour and a half on 4 cores.

**What it says.** (1) **The price steers a network as it steers a law**: extra power falls strictly across the three champions, +6.49, +0.71, -0.52 kW, and each champion beats its own start at its price by 0.030, 0.049 and 0.103 (139, 171 and 183 ids of 200). The standing H25 reading, that networks lose at every price, becomes: before being searched they lose by 0.03 to 0.10; after being searched they tie `c02` at two prices and trail it at the third. (3) **The law of the price still beats the network of the price** where the two are measured apart: p005 over n005 by 0.0123 (3.1 se) and p02 over n02 by 0.0120 (2.4 se), p04 over n04 by 0.0066 (1.2 se, a tie). At 0.005 the network buys its score with power: n005 draws +6.49 kW for dark +0.1186 and lit +0.1474 where p005 draws +2.65 kW for +0.1246 and +0.1276. (4) **The best arm at each price is the law of that price**, p005, p02, p04. (5) **The n02 champion is not a clean result.** It loses its sub in 70 of 200 dark runs and 18 of 200 lit runs, and the priced objective (the compass score and the power) does not penalise losing the sub, so the search found a pilot that sheds power partly by losing the vessel; its +0.0770 priced gain at 0.02 is a gain of a pilot that crashes in 22 percent of its runs, while every law and `c02` lose none. n04 lost one run. I did not examine what a lost run's score and power are made of; they enter the priced score as flown.
**Against the registered expectations.** A: BEATS at 0.005 (55 percent), at 0.02 (85) and at 0.04 (90): all held. B: at 0.005 TIES (my 40 percent branch; IS BEHIND 45); at 0.02 IS BEHIND (70) held; **at 0.04 TIES was my 30 percent branch** (IS BEHIND 55). C: YES (70) held. D: best at 0.005 `p005` (55) held; **at 0.02 `p02` was my 30 percent branch** (`c02` 40); at 0.04 `p04` (45) held. E: NONE (85) held. `n02`'s extra power at least 1 kW below `Wip`'s (85): held (-2.11 kW from +2.82). `n04`'s extra power below 1.0 kW (55): held (-0.52). Each champion's held-out value above `Wip`'s at its price (90 each): held (0.8478 against 0.8188, 0.7603 against 0.7183, 0.6998 against 0.5843). **The four held-out values in a group within 0.01 of each other (50 percent per group): did not hold in any group** (spreads 0.0304, 0.0304, 0.0810). Every search keeps 20 to 150 of its 600 children (70): held (81 to 148).
**What this does NOT say.** It does not say a network cannot reach the laws: one start (`Wip`), one recipe (600 iterations, 1 to 4 weights per child, sigma 0.3, no prune), four seeds per price, 13 to 27 percent of the weights moved; the seeds disagree by 0.03 to 0.08 on held-out value, so the searches are far from a settled answer, and the champion is the best of four chosen on 40 held-out ids, a selector H22 showed to be noisy. The `c02` and law comparisons are one id set. The lost-sub finding is a flaw in the OBJECTIVE, not a property of networks: a law searched without that penalty might have found the same hole (none did in H25), and a penalty for a lost sub was not part of the registered objective, so the n02 result and, to a lesser degree, the n04 result should be read as unpenalised. Nothing here says how the n02 network loses its sub (depth, floor, speed) or whether the heading score of a lost run is flattering.

## Round H27, REGISTERED (2026-11-08, before anything is built, searched or flown): A LOST SUB SCORES ZERO -- THE PRICED OBJECTIVE WITH A PENALTY, FOR THE NETWORKS AND THE LAWS
### 1. Why
H26's n02 champion lost its sub in 88 of 400 audit runs (70 dark, 18 lit), and n04's lost one; no law or network of any earlier round lost one. The priced compass-dropout objective every search since H18 used (the mean of the dark and lit scores less lambda times the mean power) scores a lost run by its heading error as flown and its power as flown, so a search can shed power by losing the vessel and be paid for it. This round closes that hole and asks what it was worth: **a lost run scores 0** (`bad`, H15's `last.lost`, is the flag), everything else unchanged. It reruns the searches of H25 (the four-weight laws, 24 searches, four prices) and of H26 (the network, 12 searches, three prices) with the SAME seeds, ids and recipes, so a search whose candidates never lose a sub reproduces its old result exactly and only the searches that touched the hole can differ.
> (A) Does the penalised network beat the unpenalised one, scored with the penalty? (B) The same for the laws. (C) Does the penalised network reach the penalised law of its price? (D) Does it reach `c02`? (E) Is any penalised champion still losing subs? (F) How many of the 36 searches changed?

### 2. The objective, the searches and the arms
**The penalised objective.** PEN(lambda) = (the mean dark score' + the mean lit score')/2 - lambda x (the mean dark power + the mean lit power)/2 in kW, where score' of a run is 0 when the run lost its sub (`bad` true) and its score otherwise; on the TRAIN ids, H15's flight, 160 s. (H18's objective with score' for score; with no lost run it IS H18's, to the last digit.)
**The law searches (H25's, 24).** The four-weight law of H16, H21's box, (1+1)-ES of 300 iterations from the zero law, step 0.1 x the box width, all four weights free, kept when at least the parent's: groups **Q005** (lambda 0.005; seeds 25001-25006), **Q01** (0.01; 25011-25016), **Q02** (0.02; 25021-25026), **Q04** (0.04; 25031-25036), train ids 25000-25023, held-out 25100-25139 (H25's, unchanged). Champion: best held-out PENALISED value at the group's price (the first of a tie in seed order): `q005`, `q01`, `q02`, `q04`.
**The network searches (H26's, 12).** The 963 weights of `network-Wip`, (1+1)-ES of 600 iterations, `PS.mutate` (1 to 4 weights, sigma 0.3), kept when at least the parent's, no prune: groups **M005** (0.005; seeds 26001-26004), **M02** (0.02; 26011-26014), **M04** (0.04; 26021-26024), train ids 26000-26023, held-out 26100-26139 (H26's, unchanged). Champions `m005`, `m02`, `m04` by held-out PENALISED value at the group's price.
**The audit:** ids **27200-27399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s, H15's flight. Eighteen arms: `autopilot`; `law0` (must equal the autopilot); `c02`; `wip`; **the unpenalised champions of the earlier rounds as stored in brain/rig-results: `p005`, `p01`, `p02`, `p04` (H25's champions by H25's rule: seeds 25003, 25016, 25024, 25031) and `n005`, `n02`, `n04` (H26's: seeds 26004, 26011, 26024)**; the penalised champions `q005`, `q01`, `q02`, `q04`, `m005`, `m02`, `m04`. Every arm is read at the three prices 0.005, 0.02, 0.04 on the PENALISED priced score: per id, (dark score' + lit score')/2 - lambda x (dark kW + lit kW)/2; PRICED GAIN its mean paired difference over the autopilot; the UNPENALISED priced gain (H25's and H26's score) is also printed.
**The verdict, registered before anything is run; fourteen parts joined by `; `. A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id on the penalised priced score at the stated price.**
**Parts A1-A3:** `AT <0.005/0.02/0.04> THE PENALISED NETWORK <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> THE UNPENALISED ONE`: `m005`/`m02`/`m04` against `n005`/`n02`/`n04`. **Parts B1-B3:** `AT <price> THE PENALISED LAW <...> THE UNPENALISED ONE`: `q005`/`q02`/`q04` against `p005`/`p02`/`p04`. **Parts C1-C3:** `AT <price> THE PENALISED NETWORK <...> THE PENALISED LAW`: `m` against `q` of the price. **Parts D1-D3:** `AT <price> THE PENALISED NETWORK <...> C02`. **Part E:** `LOST SUBS IN THE PENALISED CHAMPIONS: <the q and m champions in the order q005, q01, q02, q04, m005, m02, m04 that lost at least one of their 400 audit runs, each as name n, or NONE>`. **Part F:** `SEARCHES THAT CHANGED: <k> OF 24 LAWS, <j> OF 12 NETWORKS`: a search changed when its final weights differ from the stored unpenalised search of the same seed (H25's and H26's jsonl).
(That is A1-A3, B1-B3, C1-C3, D1-D3, E, F: fourteen.)

### 3. The checks
(a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; every network is 963 finite weights; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6**; exactly 24 law and 12 network searches, each seed once in its group; **the penalised objective equals H18's to the last digit on a flight with no lost run (a fixture on real flights) and scores a lost run 0 (a scripted fixture)**; **the first scored candidate of every network search equals `wip`'s train value at its price and every law search starts from the zero law**; each champion has the largest held-out PENALISED value at its own price in its group; the train, held-out and audit ids are disjoint; `c02`, the stored unpenalised champions and `wip` are the registered ones (their weights read from the stored files); the 8 shipped networks are not part of this round.

### 4. The lines
`LOSSES IN THE SEARCHES`: for each search the number of lost runs among the train flights of its final weights (0 almost everywhere) and whether it changed; `SEARCH Q.. / M..`: the champions' seeds and held-out penalised values; `AUDIT`: dark and lit mean score, dark power and mean dark thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `wip` and the fourteen champions; `LOST`: each arm's lost runs of its 400; `EXTRA POWER`; `PRICED GAIN` penalised and unpenalised at the three prices; `PAIRED` (the twelve pairings); `VERDICT`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** the penalised value of the unpenalised n02 (its priced gain once its 88 lost runs score 0); the Q01 and p01 comparison at 0.01; the champions' weights as a path.

### 5. Expectations, recorded BEFORE anything is run
Part F: laws changed at most 4 of 24: 70 percent (no law of H25's champions lost a sub, and a candidate that does lose one is rare); networks changed at least 4 of 12: 75 percent (a network can find the hole). Parts A: at 0.005 TIES 80, BEATS 12, IS BEHIND 8 (n005 lost nothing); at 0.02 BEATS 75, TIES 20, IS BEHIND 5 (n02 loses 88 runs, which now cost 0 each); at 0.04 TIES 55, BEATS 30, IS BEHIND 15. Parts B: TIES 80 percent at each price. Parts C: at 0.005 IS BEHIND 55, TIES 40, BEATS 5; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 60, TIES 35, BEATS 5; at 0.04 TIES 55, IS BEHIND 40, BEATS 5. Parts D: `m005` TIES 50, IS BEHIND 40, BEATS 10; `m02` IS BEHIND 65, TIES 30, BEATS 5; `m04` TIES 60, IS BEHIND 25, BEATS 15. Part E: NONE 70 percent. The penalised value of the unpenalised n02 is below `c02`'s at 0.02: 90 percent. `m02` loses fewer than 10 of its 400 audit runs: 80 percent.

### 6. What will be built
`tools/ship/headToHead27.mjs` (the penalised objective for a law arm and a network arm; the law and network searches with H25's and H26's loops reproduced exactly so that a flight with no lost run gives the old result to the last digit; the champions; the audit with the stored champions; readings at three prices on the penalised and unpenalised score; checks, verdict and lines; reads nothing from disk: the command hands in the weights, the stored searches and the new rows), `headToHead27Cmd.mjs` (`search` mode: 36 children writing a jsonl, resumable; `fly` mode: parallel parts; `--from-json`) and `headToHead27-selfcheck.mjs` (with a mutation pass, including an identity test against H25's and H26's `searchOne` on a scripted flight with no lost run). The searches are the long part: about 40 minutes for the 24 law searches and about 100 minutes for the 12 network searches, on 4 cores.

## Round H27, RESULT (2026-11-09): THE PENALTY CHANGED NO LAW AND FOUR OF TWELVE NETWORKS -- IT REMOVES THE n02 HOLE, AND THE HONEST NETWORK IS STILL 0.015 BEHIND c02 AT 0.02
Searched by `node tools/ship/headToHead27Cmd.mjs search --workers 4` (36 (1+1)-ES searches with the penalised objective, a lost sub scoring 0: the 12 network searches of H26 (600 iterations) and the 24 law searches of H25 (300), same seeds, ids and loops; about 2.3 hours on 4 cores) and flown by `... fly --parts 4` on the audit ids 27200-27399, 160 s, dark and lit, read once; all twelve checks PASS. Data: `brain/rig-results/head-to-head27-search.jsonl`, `brain/rig-results/head-to-head27.json` (1.2 MB). **Verdict as printed: `AT 0.005 THE PENALISED NETWORK TIES THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.02 THE PENALISED NETWORK BEATS THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.04 THE PENALISED NETWORK TIES THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.005 THE PENALISED LAW TIES THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.02 THE PENALISED LAW TIES THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.04 THE PENALISED LAW TIES THE UNPENALISED ONE; AT 0.005 THE PENALISED NETWORK IS BEHIND THE PENALISED LAW; AT 0.02 THE PENALISED NETWORK IS BEHIND THE PENALISED LAW; AT 0.04 THE PENALISED NETWORK TIES THE PENALISED LAW; AT 0.005 THE PENALISED NETWORK TIES C02; AT 0.02 THE PENALISED NETWORK IS BEHIND C02; AT 0.04 THE PENALISED NETWORK BEATS C02; LOST SUBS IN THE PENALISED CHAMPIONS: NONE; SEARCHES THAT CHANGED: 0 OF 24 LAWS, 4 OF 12 NETWORKS`.**
**The searches.** **No law search changed**: all 24 final laws are identical to H25's, so the four law champions (`q005` seed 25003, `q01` 25016, `q02` 25024, `q04` 25031) ARE H25's `p005`, `p01`, `p02`, `p04`, and their audit runs are identical to the digit (every paired difference 0.0000 with se 0.0000). 13 of the 24 law searches did meet a flight with a lost run among their evaluations (up to 32 flights in one 0.04 search) and none changed course. **Four of the 12 network searches changed** (26002 in M005, 26011 in M02, 26022 and 26024 in M04); the other eight are identical to H26's. Lost-run flights met by the network searches: M005 4 to 37, M02 20 to 36, M04 32 to 116. The changed searches: 26002 train 0.8759 (H26 0.8840), held 0.8279 (0.8175); **26011 train 0.7982 (0.8108), held 0.7228 (0.7603), 137 children kept (148)**; 26022 train 0.7295 (0.7521), held 0.6664 (0.6752); 26024 train 0.7529 (0.7546), held 0.6963 (0.6998). New network champions: m005 seed 26004 (held 0.8478, the H26 champion, unchanged), **m02 seed 26014 (held 0.7575, H26's runner-up: the old champion 26011 fell to 0.7228)**, m04 seed 26024 (held 0.6963, changed from 0.6998).
**The audit (dark / lit mean score; dark power; lost runs of 400).** Autopilot 0.7564 / 0.7713, 3.79 kW. `c02` 0.8643 / 0.8646, 5.08 kW. `wip` 0.8354 / 0.8569, 6.83 kW. p005 0.8782 / 0.9014, 7.34 kW. p02 0.8730 / 0.8702, 5.42 kW. p04 0.8363 / 0.8146, 3.95 kW. **n005 0.8741 / 0.9093, 11.15 kW, 0 lost. n02 0.8336 / 0.8587, 4.62 kW, 80 lost (60 dark, 20 lit). n04 0.8282 / 0.8194, 3.41 kW, 1 lost. m005 identical to n005. m02 0.8375 / 0.8486, 4.50 kW, 0 lost. m04 0.8193 / 0.8172, 3.24 kW, 0 lost.** Every law arm lost none.
**The penalised priced gain at 0.005 / 0.02 / 0.04** (and extra power). `c02` +0.0970 / +0.0861 / +0.0717 (+0.72 kW). `wip` +0.0685 / +0.0272 / -0.0279 (+2.75). p005 +0.1129 / +0.0739 / +0.0219; p01 +0.1129 / +0.0866 / +0.0515; p02 +0.1031 / +0.0893 / +0.0708; p04 +0.0635 / +0.0693 / +0.0769 (the q arms are identical). **n005 (= m005) +0.0952 / -0.0027 / -0.1331 (+6.52 kW). n02 -0.0822 / -0.0915 / -0.1040 (+0.62 kW). n04 +0.0604 / +0.0685 / +0.0793 (-0.54). m02 +0.0771 / +0.0708 / +0.0624 (+0.42). m04 +0.0577 / +0.0678 / +0.0812 (-0.67).** The UNPENALISED gains of n02 are +0.0792 / +0.0698 / +0.0573: **at 0.02 the old n02 champion is +0.0698 unpenalised and -0.0915 penalised (`c02` +0.0861)**.
**Paired on the penalised priced score at each price.** m005 minus n005 at 0.005: 0.0000 (same 200 ids; the same arm) TIES. **m02 minus n02 at 0.02: +0.1623 (se 0.0184; 128 ids better, 72 worse) BETTER.** m04 minus n04 at 0.04: +0.0018 (se 0.0030; 98 / 102) TIES. q minus p at all three prices: 0.0000 TIES. m005 minus q005 at 0.005: -0.0177 (se 0.0042; 58 / 142) WORSE. **m02 minus q02 at 0.02: -0.0185 (se 0.0030; 50 / 150) WORSE.** m04 minus q04 at 0.04: +0.0042 (se 0.0056; 107 / 93) TIES. m005 minus `c02` at 0.005: -0.0018 (se 0.0048; 90 / 110) TIES. **m02 minus `c02` at 0.02: -0.0153 (se 0.0025; 40 / 160) WORSE.** m04 minus `c02` at 0.04: +0.0095 (se 0.0044; 126 / 74) BETTER.
**What it says.** (1) **The hole was real and it was one search wide.** The H26 n02 champion (seed 26011) loses 80 of 400 audit runs (60 dark, 20 lit); scored with the penalty its priced gain at 0.02 is -0.0915 against +0.0861 for `c02`. Re-searched under the penalty, seed 26011 takes another path (held 0.7228 against 0.7603) and the champion of the group becomes seed 26014, which was already in H26's M02 group (held 0.7575) and loses no run. The other three M02 searches were unaffected. (2) **The penalty changes nothing for the four-weight laws**: not one of 24 searches ended differently, though 13 of them evaluated at least one flight with a lost run. The laws' champions never relied on losing the sub and the H25 result stands unchanged. (3) **The honest network is still behind the law of its price at 0.005 and 0.02**: m005 is 0.0177 behind q005 (4.2 se) and m02 0.0185 behind q02 (6.2 se); at 0.04 m04 ties q04 (+0.0042, 0.8 se). Against `c02`: m005 ties (-0.0018), **m02 is 0.0153 behind (6.1 se)**, m04 is ahead by 0.0095 (2.2 se) with the lowest power of any arm (-0.67 kW) and 0 lost runs. So the H26 reading, networks tie `c02` at 0.005 and 0.04 and trail it at 0.02, stands for the honest champions, with the 0.04 tie now a narrow lead. (4) The network searches meet the hole often (M04's four searches evaluated 32 to 116 flights with a lost run) yet only two of those four changed course; the hole is easiest to find at the high price, where shedding power is paid most.
**Against the registered expectations.** F: laws changed at most 4 of 24 (70 percent): held (0); networks changed at least 4 of 12 (75): held (exactly 4). A: TIES at 0.005 (80) held; BEATS at 0.02 (75) held; TIES at 0.04 (55) held. B: TIES (80 each) held at all three prices. C: at 0.005 IS BEHIND (55) held; at 0.02 IS BEHIND (60) held; at 0.04 TIES (55) held. D: m005 TIES (50) held; m02 IS BEHIND (65) held; **m04 BEATS `c02` was my 15 percent branch** (TIES 60). E: NONE (70) held. The unpenalised n02's penalised value below `c02`'s at 0.02 (90): held (-0.0915 against +0.0861). m02 loses fewer than 10 of its 400 audit runs (80): held (0).
**What this does NOT say.** It does not say a lost run should score exactly 0: that is one penalty among several (excluding the run, charging its power only), and the lost runs' own heading scores before the loss were never examined, so the unpenalised flattery in H26 may be partly an artefact of how a lost run is scored and not only of the loss. It does not say the networks cannot reach the laws: one start, four seeds per price, 600 iterations, and the m02 champion is the runner-up of H26's group on 40 held-out ids. The 0.04 lead of m04 over `c02` is 2.2 se on one id set. Seeds that did not change (eight of twelve) are the same results as H26 on H26's ids, not independent confirmations. The penalised objective sees a lost run only at the END of the flight (`last.lost`); a sub lost and recovered mid-flight is not penalised.

## Round H28, REGISTERED (2026-11-09, before anything is built, searched or flown): THE PENALISED NETWORK SEARCH FROM TWO OTHER STARTS
### 1. Why
Every network result since H26 starts from one place, `network-Wip`. H27's m04 is ahead of `c02` at 0.04 by 0.0095 (2.2 se), and m02 trails `c02` at 0.02 by 0.0153: each is ONE champion of four searches from ONE start. This round asks whether those readings belong to the network or to the start. The penalised objective, the prices, the iteration budget and the seeds' structure are H27's, unchanged; **only the start changes**.
> (A) Does a network searched from another start reach the same value as the Wip-started one at its price? (B) Does m04's lead over `c02` at 0.04 reproduce from either new start? (C) Does any start reach the law of its price? (D) Does any new champion lose subs?

### 2. The starts, the searches and the arms
**Two new starts (both 963 weights, `pilotSubPolicy` GFC, `pilot:"network", circuit:"gfc"`):** **ACT** = `brain/pilotSubWeightsActuatorsWip.json` (Wip trained under rate limits; sum -2.21805099141784, sumsq 289.3828709763427; it differs from Wip in 175 of 963 weights, so it is a NEIGHBOUR of Wip, not a stranger); **HND** = `handWeights()` of the same policy (the autopilot written through the network; sum 0, sumsq 248.71788106166872; a different start altogether). Fingerprints are checked at the start of every run; Wip's is H26's.
**The searches (24):** H27's penalised network search exactly (the 963 weights, (1+1)-ES of 600 iterations, `PS.mutate` 1 to 4 weights, sigma 0.3, kept when at least the parent's, no prune, penalised priced objective on the TRAIN ids, H15's flight, 160 s). Groups **A005 / A02 / A04** (ACT start; prices 0.005 / 0.02 / 0.04; seeds 28001-28004 / 28011-28014 / 28021-28024) and **H005 / H02 / H04** (HND start; same prices; seeds 28031-28034 / 28041-28044 / 28051-28054). Train ids 26000-26023 and held-out ids 26100-26139 (H26's and H27's, unchanged: the arms of H27 are therefore comparable on the held-out set). Champion of a group: best held-out PENALISED value at the group's price (the first of a tie in seed order): `a005`, `a02`, `a04`, `h005`, `h02`, `h04`.
**The audit:** ids **28200-28399 (200), read once**, dark and lit, 160 s. Arms (16; registered as "14" by a miscount, corrected before anything was run): `autopilot`; `law0` (must equal the autopilot); `c02`; `wip`; **H27's penalised champions read from `brain/rig-results/head-to-head27.json`: `q005`, `q02`, `q04` (the laws of the price) and `m005`, `m02`, `m04`** (the Wip-started networks); the six new champions. Each arm is read at the three prices 0.005, 0.02, 0.04 on the PENALISED priced score (per id (dark score' + lit score')/2 - lambda x (dark kW + lit kW)/2, score' = 0 for a lost run); PRICED GAIN its mean paired difference over the autopilot.
**The verdict, registered before anything is run; fourteen parts joined by `; ` (registered as "fifteen" by a miscount, corrected before anything was run). A reading is the switchboard's at 2 se (BETTER = BEATS, WORSE = IS BEHIND, else TIES), paired by id on the penalised priced score at the stated price.**
**Parts A1-A3:** `AT <price> THE ACT NETWORK <BEATS/IS BEHIND/TIES> THE WIP NETWORK`: `a` against `m` of the price. **Parts B1-B3:** `AT <price> THE HND NETWORK <...> THE WIP NETWORK`: `h` against `m`. **Parts C1-C3:** `AT <price> THE BETTER OF THE NEW NETWORKS <...> THE LAW OF THE PRICE`: the new champion with the larger audit penalised priced gain at that price (the first of a tie: `a`), against `q`. **Parts D1-D3:** `AT <price> THE BETTER OF THE NEW NETWORKS <...> C02`. **Part E:** `LOST SUBS IN THE NEW CHAMPIONS: <those of a005, a02, a04, h005, h02, h04 that lost at least one of their 400 audit runs, each as name n, or NONE>`. **Part F:** `M04'S LEAD OVER C02 AT 0.04 REPRODUCES FROM <the starts, in the order ACT, HND, whose champion a04 / h04 BEATS c02 at 0.04, or NEITHER>`.
(That is A1-A3, B1-B3, C1-C3, D1-D3, E, F: fourteen.)

### 3. The checks
(thirteen rows; a failure and the verdict are NOT WEIGHED): every arm flew 200 ids in both conditions; ids aligned; no score outside (0, 1); every power and thrust finite, power at least 200 W; the autopilot's dark and lit scores differ; every network is 963 finite weights; **`law0` equals the autopilot in score, power and thrust to 1e-6**; exactly 24 searches, each seed once in its group; **the three start fingerprints are the registered ones**; **the first scored candidate of every search equals its start's penalised train value at the group's price**; each champion has the largest held-out PENALISED value at its own price in its group; the train, held-out and audit ids are disjoint from each other (audit disjoint from every earlier audit range 22200-27399); `c02`, `wip` and the H27 champions are the registered ones (read from stored files, fingerprints compared); **H27's penalised objective is reused unchanged (a fixture: on a flight with no lost run it equals H18's, and a lost run scores 0)**; the 8 shipped networks are not part of this round.

### 4. The lines
`SEARCH A.. / H..`: the champions' seeds and held-out penalised values, and each search's train start and end value; `LOSSES IN THE SEARCHES`: lost train runs of each search's final weights; `AUDIT`: dark and lit mean score, dark power and dark thrust of the autopilot, `c02`, `wip`, the `q` and `m` arms and the six new champions; `LOST`: each arm's lost runs of its 400; `EXTRA POWER`; `PRICED GAIN` penalised at the three prices; `PAIRED`; `VERDICT`. **REPORTED, NOT IN THE VERDICT:** how far each new champion's weights sit from its start and from `wip` (L1 and number changed); the spread of held-out values across the four seeds of every group (is the group's champion a typical or a lucky search?); the unpenalised priced gain.

### 5. Expectations, recorded BEFORE anything is run
Parts A (ACT against Wip): TIES 65, BEATS 15, IS BEHIND 20 at each price (ACT is a neighbour of Wip). Parts B (HND against Wip): at 0.005 IS BEHIND 45, TIES 40, BEATS 15; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 40, TIES 40, BEATS 20; at 0.04 TIES 40, IS BEHIND 35, BEATS 25 (the hand start is far from where Wip's search began and 600 iterations may not close the gap). Parts C: at 0.005 IS BEHIND 60, TIES 35, BEATS 5; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 60, TIES 35, BEATS 5; at 0.04 TIES 50, IS BEHIND 40, BEATS 10. Parts D: at 0.005 IS BEHIND 45, TIES 45, BEATS 10; at 0.02 IS BEHIND 60, TIES 35, BEATS 5; at 0.04 TIES 45, BEATS 30, IS BEHIND 25. Part E: NONE 65 percent. Part F: m04's lead reproduces from NEITHER start 40 percent, from exactly one 40 percent, from both 20 percent (my prior is that the start matters: the lead is one champion at 2.2 se). The new champions' held-out values spread across the four seeds of a group by more than 0.01 in at least four of the six groups: 60 percent. `h`'s champions sit farther (L1) from their start than `a`'s do from theirs: 90 percent.

### 6. What will be built
`tools/ship/headToHead28.mjs` (reuses H27's `penObjective`, `netSearch`, `netChampion` and readings through import; adds the start table with fingerprints, the six groups, the audit with the stored H27 champions, the fifteen-part verdict, checks and lines; reads nothing from disk), `headToHead28Cmd.mjs` (`search` mode: 24 children writing a jsonl, resumable; `fly` mode: parallel parts; `--from-json`) and `headToHead28-selfcheck.mjs` (with a mutation pass, including an identity test that a Wip-start search through H28's code equals H27's `netSearch` on a scripted flight). Cost: the 24 network searches are the long part, about 200 minutes on 4 cores (H27's 12 took about 100). Not in this round: other penalties, longer searches, a finer price grid.

### 7. What this round does NOT say
It does not test the penalty's form, search length or the price grid. ACT is a near neighbour of Wip, so agreement between them is weak evidence of start-independence; HND is the real test. Four seeds per group is a champion of four, as before.

### 8. ADDENDUM (2026-11-09, DURING THE RUN, before the failed search is rerun and before the flight): A CANDIDATE THAT LOSES ITS SUB BEFORE THE DROPOUT CRASHED H005 SEED 28032
H15's flight scores a dark run on the ticks where the compass reads dark, and throws when there is none. Seed 28032's candidate (the 947th flight of that search, train id 26012) loses its sub at 9.1 s, respawns seven times in 160 s, and each respawn comes back with no compass reading, so the dropout never registers and the flight threw; the search died (exit 1). Reproduced and traced (`brain/rig-results/head-to-head28-lostcase.json` holds the candidate). **The repair, made in H28's own code and not in H15's** (`flyPen` in `headToHead28.mjs`): a run that has no scoreable tick AND whose sub was lost is returned as a lost run (`bad` true), its heading error read over every tick (a number inside (0, pi), as H27's lost runs have), its power, speed and thrust as flown; under the penalty it scores 0 like every lost run. A run that has no scoreable tick and was NOT lost still throws, and so does every other error. On every run H15's flight scores, `flyPen` returns the same run, so a search that never met the case (all 16 that had finished, and every one that finishes without a refusal) is what it would have been; only the searches that crashed are rerun. This is a change to the registered recipe's flight (a lost run with no dark tick had no defined score), made after the run began, and it is disclosed here rather than hidden. The ACT searches (12) and H005 seeds 28031, 28033, 28034 ran under the old code and are kept; seed 28032 and everything not yet finished run under the new. Nothing in the verdict, the audit ids or the expectations changes. If a further search crashes for a reason other than this one, it is reported, not repaired silently.
